Publication Date: 1781 (title page, read directly; the host record says 1780)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1781); the archive.org record carries no rights field, so the imprint date is the basis
Language: fra
Source: archive.org item lettresdifiant_p20jesu, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-10 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (661,139) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1781, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: fair. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: Volume 20 of the collection, of the China mission: the Jesuits' own letters from China. The first letter is dated at Peking, 20 July 1725, at about OCR line 97.
